PRELIMINARY RESEARCH EVIDENCE MAP — PENDING COMPLETION
This is a searchable plain-text extraction of the 12-tab workbook G1_Continuous_Review_193_87_Studies_Audited.xlsx, NOT the lossless XLSX binary. Some tabular cell formats and formulas may not be preserved. The workbook reports 87 preliminary study entries from a 193-record worklist; these are not 87 fully verified full-text studies. No claim of MVA therapeutic efficacy. An Excel-format reconstruction of the 12-sheet workbook is now uploaded in the 'Research into scoping review' GitHub directory. The separately retained source workbook may differ in formatting.

TAB NAME: Eligibility and audit rules>index,Criterion,Operational definition / rule
0,Review scope,"Niacin, NMN, and 4-PBA tested experimentally in genetic disease models"
1,Study types,"Original experimental research (human, animal, in vitro, ex vivo)"
2,Exclude,"Plant experiments, systematic reviews, meta-analyses, narrative reviews, case reports, unreported intervention, no measured outcome"
3,Outcome direction,"Include negative, null, mixed and positive findings if they satisfy scope"
4,Article-body reviewed,Article methods/results sections available in cited source; not proof that all experimental arms independently checked
5,Abstract/publisher preview only,Initial evidence note; still pending formal full-text eligibility decision
6,Not obtained,"Set aside for retrieval, not counted as scientific exclusion"
7,MVA relevance,Indirect genetic-disease findings do not demonstrate BUB1B or MVA rescue
8,Source link,"For source verification, not clinical treatment instruction"
9,Published exposure conditions,Not reproduced in this version; separate validation from source Methods required
10,Audit status,AI-assisted preliminary review: scientific and bibliographic confirmation required<PARSED TEXT FOR SHEET: 6 / 12 TABS
